# Skills · O · part 38

985 entries, ontology-creator to oo-ftshare. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **ontology-creator**: Builds a structured ontology, taxonomy, or knowledge graph from a collection of brain atoms, work products, or text inputs. Extracts entities, defines relationships between them, and maps the conceptual structure of a domain as it has accumulated in the brain. Used by quality-control and innovator to understand what the brain actually knows — and where its knowledge map has gaps or structural asymmetries. ([Clvrwrk/a-roofer-open-brain](https://github.com/Clvrwrk/a-roofer-open-brain/tree/HEAD/skills/infranodus/ontology-creator))
- **ontology-curator**: Manages ontology maintenance, evolution, and versioning. Handles deprecation of ontology terms, KGCL change management, ontology diff generation, and ontology release workflows. Use when maintaining, updating, versioning, or documenting an existing OWL/RDF ontology. ([beep-effect/beep-effect](https://github.com/beep-effect/beep-effect/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ontology-curator) · ★80)
- **ontology-curator**: Maintains existing ontologies through classified KGCL change proposals, term deprecation, reparenting, and renaming; import refresh against the imports-manifest; diff generation; versioning; release workflows (preparation + release notes + FAIR / OBO publication: PURL, content negotiation). Owns KGCL change management and the maintenance / evolution of ontology artifacts. Use for maintaining, updating, versioning, documenting, deprecating, renaming, reparenting, refreshing imports, preparing release workflows, or communicating ontology changes to consumers. ([mepuka/ontology-skill](https://github.com/mepuka/ontology-skill/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/ontology-curator) · ★1)
- **ontology-curator**: Plans a change to a term of this repository's ERP ontology (the explorations/erp-ontology packet): renaming, re-parenting or retiring a term with its replacement, bumping the ontology's version, or writing its release notes, as a proposal for Benjamin in a research report. Use when an ontology term or the ontology's version must change. Not for renaming or removing app records, database columns, schema fields or code. ([kriegcloud/inventory-import](https://github.com/kriegcloud/inventory-import/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/ontology-curator))
